跨國鎮壓 / Transnational Repression

Tier E event record. The community is a party to this: people of Taiwanese and Hong Kong origin living in the United States, Canada and Europe are surveilled, coerced, or attacked on behalf of the PRC state. This page is not commentary on the news — it is the archive’s own record of a pattern we have documented since 2021, assembled from our own holdings so that any future story lands on a spine we already have.

從每日脈絡來的話

這頁不是今天的新聞。每日脈絡把你送到這裡，是因為今天的報導用了「跨國鎮壓」。點進來應看到兩件事：我們從 2021 年就有的紀錄，以及今天這幾則報導各自落在哪個已有的形狀上。下面每一則今天的消息都標 報導，不是我們核對過的檔案。

2026-10-26 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

Reported (off-spine arrival — ruled Tier D, kept off this spine’s arrival section). 2026-10-26 — 美國進口車0關稅倒數？行政院最新說法出爐 | 三立新聞網 SETN.COM setn.com/news/1847120, 記者 鍾釗榛／台北報導, 發布 2026-05-29 15:23（更新 15:25） (wire) — needs-tier t_f434fbbf. Body read in full in-browser from the live setn URL (the Google News redirect returned “Content was inaccessible or not found” via web_extract — the t_88e678ef read-text rule). Form matters for the ruling: this is a car-market/trade-policy item, not a repression event. Substance retained, all of it the quoted officials’ as reported: 行政院副院長鄭麗君 says 美國進口車零關稅 must wait for the US 301 調查結果; only then will 行政院 send the 台美對等貿易協定（ART）與備忘錄（MOU）to 立法院 for 審查; the 15% 對等關稅 on 台灣汽車零組件 applies first because it is 載明 in the MOU (no extra MFN or 半導體 232 疊加); 232 條款 not yet formally published; after a US court ruled the original tariff legal basis 失效 in February, the US moved to 122 條款 暫時課徵 10% + MFN (~150 days) while running the 301 調查, result estimated 最快 7 月底; Tesla/Ford 在台售價 is the market watch point. Labeling: every characterization is the 行政院’s / the paper’s; nothing here is a finding of fact by this archive. Tier ruling: Tier D — cite reporting, no page mint, no arrival entry on this spine (recorded here in Revision History only, the t_e0fcfd3f Okinawa precedent). Party test (CONTROL §1b) fails every limb: the venue is 行政院/立法院 in Taipei, the subjects are ROC officials legislating ROC tariff law, the affected parties are 台灣 consumers and US brands’ Taiwan pricing, and there is no 台美人 target, nothing on U.S./Canadian soil against a person or organization we hold, and no families-surveilled-here claim. Theme = 台美經貿, not 跨國鎮壓; the card fired NEEDS-TIER on named-subject matching, not on theme. Reported. 2026-10-26 — 中國跨國鎮壓 官員：在台港人若配合 廢止居留定居 自由時報電子報 政治 (wire) — needs-tier t_18f4ac65. 自由時報電子報（LINE TODAY 分發面），記者 陳鈺馥／台北報導，紙本頁發布 2026-10-26 05:30；網路版同文先行：「內幕》在台港人若配合中國跨境鎮壓 官員：將廢止居留定居」breakingnews 5618035, 2026-10-25 12:46（同記者、同內容）。Both bodies read in full from the two live ltn.com.tw URLs (the Google News redirect returned “Content was inaccessible or not found” via web_extract — the t_88e678ef read-text rule; canonical URLs resolved by search). Form matters for the ruling: this is an anonymous-official briefing leg of one prosecution we already hold, not a new proceeding. Substance retained, all of it the quoted 知情官員’s as reported: (1) 北檢已起訴 4 名台嫌、對港籍僱主 鞠覺賢、林慶 發布通緝，二港人「未在台灣設籍」，未來若有在台港人配合中共跨境鎮壓，「已經入籍的，會註銷其台灣身分，還在居留階段，不會讓他們定居」— the 廢止居留定居 policy signal is the only new content in this copy; (2) 「這二名港人並不是在台灣居留的港人，明顯有集團在背後跨國指揮」，委託的香港人才是重點，因為是跨境鎮壓的來源; (3) 台港司法互助幾乎停頓、港府不會交人，引渡有困難，故制度目標是「抓得到、判得重」，在出境前逮捕到案; (4) 法務部正研議修正《反滲透法》加重其刑，境外鎮壓犯罪不應再視為個人法益、須視為侵犯國家法益才能判重刑，否則現在都是違反個資法等輕罪; (5) 對照例：印太戰略智庫執行長 矢板明夫 在台中遭暴力攻擊、行兇者也是香港人、檢方起訴五年（「揍一拳就5年」，不再視為單純傷害案），以及「中共派女房仲在美國跟拍、蒐集賴總統家人的資訊，在美國這種罪判很重」。Labeling: every characterization above is the anonymous 知情官員’s (unnamed, so no actor row — t_c143aa9a precedent); 起訴 remains allegation (未判決、推定無罪), 通緝 is a prosecutorial measure not a finding; 「境外敵對勢力」「跨國指揮」「有集團在背後」are the official’s framing carried by the paper, and our own 安保協會 record still notes 未證實 港籍委託人與中國官方機關間的具體指揮關係. Nothing in this copy is a finding of fact by this archive.

Wanying Zhang, aka “Heather” (18 U.S.C. § 951), already carried under t_83a964c2 (2026-10-14), t_7979cbe0 (10-17), t_bca025f2 + t_35ee8f07 (10-19), t_2a804da6 (10-20), t_928b9374 (10-20). The Google News redirect is inaccessible (t_88e678ef read-text rule), and INQUI’s own URL served a Cloudflare interstitial (Just a moment..., 5.8 KB) while The Online Citizen 403’d, so the body was read in full from CBS News (Josh Boswell, CBS News Investigative Unit, 2026-10-23 5:15 PM EDT, cbsnews.com/news/wanying-zhang-alleged-chinese-agent-pleads-not-guilty/) and NBC Los Angeles (Helen Jeong, Danielle Smith & AP, 2026-10-23, nbclosangeles.com/news/local/irvine-spy-china-fbi-lax/3960062/), which carry the same event and the same quotes as the INQUI wire. What this filing advances is the court posture, and only the court posture: Zhang pleaded not guilty on Friday 2026-10-23 at the post-indictment arraignment in federal court in downtown Los Angeles — the step our pages carried as scheduled, now held. She appeared in a white jumpsuit and shackles, speaking only to deny the charges. The judge set a pretrial hearing for 2026-11-12 and trial to begin 2026-11-30 at the federal courthouse in Santa Ana, estimated to last four days — dates that were not in the archive before this filing. Both prosecutors and defense counsel Craig Harbaugh declined to comment. Husband Edric Ding (realtor at the same Irvine firm) had appeared at earlier hearings and did not appear Friday, after prosecutors claimed in a recent filing that Zhang was having an affair with the man who accompanied her on the alleged Washington-state surveillance; CBS again names him as Fatai Miao, not charged, who left Southern California on a one-way ticket to China, and whose son denies any wrongdoing. NBC/AP add the Presidential Office statement attributed by name to spokesperson Karen Kuo (「a classic case of transnational repression」, 「Taiwan condemns in the strongest terms such barbaric acts…」) and the Chinese Foreign Ministry’s reply (not aware of the incident; opposes 「hyped-up accusations of ‘China spying’」). Labeling unchanged: indictment = allegation, no finding of fact, defendant presumed innocent until proven guilty (推定無罪); every characterization above is the speaker’s own, attributed.

2026-10-22 — 報導，尚未成為我們的檔案

Reported. 2026-10-22 — 中國民主黨國際聯盟揭中共20多宗跨國鎮壓 大紀元 (wire) — 大紀元記者陶若水紐約報導 2026-10-22 09:24 AM, n14874264, 責任編輯 鄭樺. Body read in full from the live epochtimes b5 URL (the Google News redirect is JS-only — the t_88e678ef read-text rule; URL resolved by search, and the 阿波羅新聞網 syndication carries the identical text, author 陶若水, 責任編輯 李華 — publisher-side editor drift, not a text variant). This is NOT a sibling of the 《涉政落查人員庫》 cluster already on this spine under t_e58ab73c / t_8dbfccc6 / t_c53fb57f: that story is the leaked 24,170-record database; this one is a street action — 中國民主黨國際聯盟 (CDPIA) held a rally on 2026-10-18 in front of the Queens Library in Flushing (王后區法拉盛圖書館前), a 華人-concentrated public venue, with display boards showing photographs of Chinese police visiting members’ families, footage of the assault on 民運人士 at the earlier Washington protest, and phone/微信 threat recordings played over a loudspeaker to passers-by.

What the item alleges (all attributed; no finding of fact). 主席 界立建 says that around the September 2026 visit to the U.S. by 習近平 and the organization’s Washington protest, the alliance holds evidence of 20+ cases of intimidation, threats and harassment against members and their families; some were threatened before departure, gave up protesting, and were harassed anyway. Named first-person accounts: 徐其仁 (江西) supplied a call recording in which local 國保 personnel, having taken his mother to a 中共社區機構 and phoning him in front of her, said they knew of his CDPIA membership and his dealings with 界立建, demanded he withdraw, referenced his pregnant wife, warned his mother could be implicated and that he faced imprisonment if he returned to China. 張劉敏 (河南鄭州) and 方碩 (江蘇) each received threat calls before the Washington trip — 張劉敏 threatened with her child’s safety — both stood down, were harassed regardless, and were told to leave the alliance and cut contact with 界立建 or 「後果自負」; 張劉敏 said she would not withdraw and would publicize the case and pursue legal accountability. 界立建 said the harassment is not confined to families in China: the alliance’s Malaysia web administrator 楊佳 was followed and threatened and dare not return to his residence, and members in Thailand feared rendition to China. Rally attendee 李中平 said Flushing was chosen precisely because it is 華人集中, and called on the U.S. government to investigate CCP agents operating illegally in the U.S. and to protect threatened people. 界立建 said the alliance is collecting names, recordings and video to submit to the U.S. government, Congress and international human-rights bodies under the Global Magnitsky Act, and urged targets to report and preserve evidence.

Tier ruling — Tier D for every named subject; no page minted. The CONTROL §1b discriminator is party status, and it returns Tier D here even though the venue is a U.S.-soil 華人 public space and the theme is genuinely ours: the people named as targets are members of a Chinese-dissident organization, their surveilled families are in mainland China (江西, 河南鄭州, 江蘇), and the two cross-border legs are Malaysia and Thailand — not 台美人 subjects, not families surveilled here. The U.S.-soil element in this item is the rally, not the repression. No 台美人 target, no action against any person or organization we record, no families-surveilled-here claim. Precedents: t_c53fb57f (界立建 as quoted interviewee), t_c0953057 / t_63ba370b (non-U.S.-soil legs of this theme), t_50d337d5 / t_c143aa9a (no pages for unnamed groups).

Why this belongs in Echopedia

Per CONTROL §1b, the Tier D↔E discriminator is whether the community is a party, not whether the story is famous. It is:

  • The targets are members of, or adjacent to, the diaspora we record — 台灣人 abroad, 僑界, Hong Kong residents in U.S. cities, and family members of public figures.
  • The operations occur on soil where our members live: Los Angeles, the San Gabriel Valley, Toronto, university towns across the U.S.
  • Our archive carried the evidence before the current cycle made it a headline.

Labeling standard: every claim below carries its status. Sourced = a document in this archive. Reported = press assertion we have not independently verified. Alleged = accusation in a filing or statement, recorded with attribution and without a finding of fact. Nothing is omitted for being uncomfortable; nothing is asserted beyond what the record holds.

The pattern, as our own records show it

Sourced. 強制送中，國際組織：中國在全世界「獵捕」台灣人 records international organizations’ finding that Taiwan nationals are pursued abroad. The framing is not “influence” but capture.

2022 — religious communities targeted under 天網 / 獵狐

Sourced. 中國通過”天網""獵狐”跨國鎮壓宗教團體成員 — the operation against religious-group members abroad, filed under the 天網/獵狐 framework; the archive’s own title records 美國際宗教自由委員會 naming 習近平 in its criticism.

2023 — the legislative response begins

Sourced. 美兩黨參議員提案打擊中國跨境鎮壓行動 — bipartisan Senate action, i.e. this is a recognized state-to-state problem, not an immigrant-community rumor.

Sourced. 加拿大警方稱已關閉與”中國警察站”有關的”非法警務活動” — Toronto. Physical police stations operated on Canadian soil.

Sourced. 「不支持言論自由，就別來上學」普渡大學校長公開信 — the campus vector: students harassing peers over political speech, with the pressure traced to PRC-aligned organizing.

2024 — sanctions as the countermeasure; retaliation against individuals

Sourced. 許穎婷推制裁法案觸怒港府 遭懸賞百萬通緝 — a legislator of our region facing a bounty. This is the community as a party in the most literal sense.

Sourced. 習近平訪美期間許多抗議人士被圍毆 — physical assault on protesters during a state visit; human-rights groups sought hearings.

2025 — the multilateral layer

Sourced. 跨國調查揭北京鎮壓滲透全球 聯合國淪中國打壓異己工具 — an investigative body of work, not one outlet’s account.

Sourced. 香港通過基本法23條立法 跨國近80位政界人士譴責

Sourced. 區域宗教自由論壇：台灣是中共遂行「再教育」下一目標

Sourced. 美國務院報告：中共戰狼及假帳號充斥網絡空間 — the information-operation layer that precedes and accompanies physical action.

NEED YOU

The archive’s coverage of this theme is real but thin between 2021 and 2023, and absent on the 2026 legislative cycle. If you were in Toronto, Los Angeles, or a campus community during any of these operations — your first-hand account is the primary source we do not have. One sentence is enough.
